\displaystyle{\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}

\displaystyle=\lambda{W}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}{\mathcal{X}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}+\lambda{W}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{\mathcal{X}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\mathcal{X}}.

Additionally, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)

(4.25)

\|{{\Omega_{L}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

Note that
{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}$}}}
is already controlled by Lemma

4.2
. Also, the expressions for the cohomological operator applied to
each block of
{\Omega_{L}}
follow directly from (
4.24
).
Additionally,
{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}$}}}
is trivially zero and, in
the case of
1-
dimensional bundles,
{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}
is also trivially zero.
We also need to show that
{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}
has zero average and,
consequently, so does
{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}
. Observe that

{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}=({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\big(X{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})-{\rm D}_{\varphi}K\hat{\alpha}\big).

For the first term, if we use that
\Omega(z)X(z,\varphi)={\rm D}_{z}H(z,\varphi)^{\!\top\!}
,
we obtain

\left\langle({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\top}\big({\rm D}_{z}H{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\top}\right\rangle=\Big\langle\Big({\rm D}_{\theta}\big(H{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)\Big)^{\!\top\!}\Big\rangle=0,

since we are taking averages of derivatives with respect to
\theta
.
The second term, using that
\boldsymbol{\omega}
is exact, reads

\Big\langle({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}{\rm D}_{\varphi}(a{\!\>\circ\!\>}K)-\big({\rm D}_{\theta}(a{\!\>\circ\!\>}K)\big)^{\!\top\!}{\rm D}_{\varphi}K\Big\rangle\hat{\alpha}.

Then, each entry
i
of
{\langle{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\rangle}
is obtained from

\displaystyle{\langle{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\rangle}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}\Big\langle\sum_{k}\partial_{\theta^{i}}K^{k}\partial_{\varphi^{j}}(a_{k}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K)-\partial_{\theta^{i}}(a_{k}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K)\partial_{\varphi^{j}}K^{k}\Big\rangle\hat{\alpha}^{j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}\Big\langle\sum_{k}\partial_{\varphi^{j}}\big(a_{k}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K\partial_{\theta^{i}}K^{k}\big)-a_{k}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K\partial^{2}_{\theta^{i}\varphi^{j}}K^{k}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}-\partial_{\theta^{i}}\big(a_{k}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K\partial_{\varphi^{j}}K^{k}\big)+a_{k}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K\partial^{2}_{\theta^{i}\varphi^{j}}K^{k}\Big\rangle\hat{\alpha}^{j}

\displaystyle=0

since we are taking averages of derivatives with respect to

\theta
and
\varphi
.
For estimate (
4.25
), note first that

(4.26)

\displaystyle\|{\Omega_{L}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\max\Big\{

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta},

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}+\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta},

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}+\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}+\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}\Big\}

and that
{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}
is well-defined by assumption (
4.12
).
We proceed then by obtaining estimates for each block. Let us
first estimate

(4.27)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathfrak{L}}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}{C_{{\mathcal{X}}}}\delta+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\mathcal{X}}$}}+2nc_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}{C_{{\mathcal{X}}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.28)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\delta+2nc_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\right)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.29)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathfrak{L}}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left({C_{{{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}\delta+{C_{{{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}d+C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.30)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left({C_{{{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\delta+C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+\left({C_{{{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\right)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.31)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}\delta+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}d\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+\left(2nc_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}\right)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

(4.32)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}{C_{{\mathcal{X}}}}\delta+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\mathcal{X}}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+\left(2nc_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}{C_{{\mathcal{X}}}}\right)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

where we used Lemma
4.1
.

We are now in the position to use Rüssmann estimates (
2.10
) and
(
2.8
) and obtain

(4.33)

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\mathfrak{R}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.34)

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}^{K}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.35)

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\mathfrak{R}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.36)

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}^{K}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.37)

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}^{K}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.38)

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\mathfrak{L}}^{1\lambda}{\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}^{K}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Lastly, from (
4.26
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|{{\Omega_{L}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{\left(C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\max\Big\{}\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\max\Big\{}\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}^{K}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}^{K}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}^{W}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}^{W}}\right)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\max\Big\{}\Big({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}^{K}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}^{K}}\Big)\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Big\}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\max\Big\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\!K}\!{W}$}}}$}}^{W}},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\mathcal{X}}{W}$}}}$}}^{{W}}},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}{{\rm D}\!K}$}}}$}}^{W}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}\!{\mathcal{X}}$}}}$}}^{W}}\Big\}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

∎

4.5.
Approximate symplecticity of the frame
{\hat{P}}

Let
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{2n\times 2n}
denote the error in the
symplecticity of
{\hat{P}}
. That is,

(4.39)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}:={\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}-{\Omega_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}{L}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}{L}&{L}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\hat{N}+I_{n}\\
\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}{L}-I_{n}&\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\hat{N}\end{pmatrix}.

Lemma 4.4
.

The symplecticity error
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}

is given by

(4.40)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}{\Omega_{L}}&\\
\hline\cr&\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{${B}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega_{L}}{B}$}\end{array}\right).

Additionally, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)

(4.41)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

Let us compute

\displaystyle{L}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\hat{N}

\displaystyle={L}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big(\hat{N}+{L}{A}\Big)=-I_{n},

\displaystyle\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}{L}

\displaystyle=I_{n},

\displaystyle\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\hat{N}

\displaystyle={B}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega_{L}}{B},

where we used (
3.5
). From these expressions and the definition of

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}
, (
4.40
) follows directly. In order to obtain
estimate (
4.41
) notice that

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\|{{\Omega_{L}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\max\Big\{1,\ \|{B}\|_{\rho}^{2}\Big\}.

We can now use Lemma
4.3
to obtain

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\Omega_{L}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\max\Big\{1,\ ({\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}})^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\max\Big\{1,\ ({\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}})^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

∎

4.6.
Approximate reducibility of the frame

{\hat{P}}

Let us define the error in the reducibility of the linearized
dynamics in the coordinates given by the frame
{\hat{P}}

as
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{2n\times 2n}
given by

(4.42)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}:=-{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\hat{P}}-\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}\Lambda&\hat{S}\\
\hline\cr&\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{$\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}$}\end{array}\right).

First, let

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}=\begin{pmatrix}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}&E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{12}\\
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}&E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}\end{pmatrix}

where

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}

\displaystyle=\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){L}-\Lambda,

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{12}

\displaystyle=\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}-\hat{S},

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}

\displaystyle=-{L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){L},

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}

\displaystyle=-{L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}-\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}.

Lemma 4.5
.

The reducibility error
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}
,
is given by

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}

\displaystyle=\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{L},

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{12}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}_{n}

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}

\displaystyle=-{\Omega_{L}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Lambda-{L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{L},

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}

\displaystyle={L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}E_{L}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}.

Additionally, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)

(4.43)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

First, recall the definition (
3.7
) for
\hat{S}
. Then,

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{12}
is identically zero. For
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}
,
let us use (
4.11
) to obtain

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}

\displaystyle=\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\left({L}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Lambda+E_{L}\right)-\Lambda

\displaystyle=\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{L}

Similarly, for
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}
we have

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}

\displaystyle=-{L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){L}

\displaystyle=-{L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\left({L}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Lambda+E_{L}\right)

\displaystyle=-{\Omega_{L}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Lambda-{L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{L}.

Lastly, using the symplecticity of
\phi_{T}
,
and (
4.11
) we have

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}

\displaystyle=-\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}L^{{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}E_{L}^{{\!\top\!}}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}-\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}

\displaystyle=\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}{L}^{{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}E_{L}^{{\!\top\!}}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle={L}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}E_{L}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}.

For estimate (
4.43
), first note that

{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}
is well-defined by assumption (
4.12
), that

\displaystyle\|{\Lambda}\|

\displaystyle={\max{\left\{1,\ |{\lambda}|\right\}}}\leq{\max{\left\{1,\ \sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}\right\}}}=1,

\displaystyle\|{\Lambda^{-1}}\|

\displaystyle={\max{\left\{1,\ |{\lambda^{\!-1}}|\right\}}}\leq{\max{\left\{1,\ \sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}\right\}}}=\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}},

and

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq{\max{\left\{\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},\ \|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\right\}}}.

We proceed then by computing estimates for each block

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{ij}
as

(4.44)

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{L}$}}^{K}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{L}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}$}}^{K}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.45)

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\Big({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{K}}+{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{L}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}+\Big({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Omega_{L}}$}}^{{W}}}+{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{L}$}}^{W}}\Big)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(4.46)

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}}\|_{\rho-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\Big({C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\delta+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}^{K}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}

\displaystyle\phantom{<}+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}^{W}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}$}}^{K}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

where we used Lemmas
4.1
and
4.3
.
Finally,

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}$}}^{K}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\delta}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},\ \Big({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}$}}^{K}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\Big({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}$}}^{{W}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}$}}^{{W}}}\Big)\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Big\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}$}}^{K}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Big\}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\max\Big\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{11}$}}^{{W}}},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{21}$}}^{{W}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22}$}}^{{W}}}\Big\}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

∎

4.7.
Approximate invertibility of
{\hat{P}}

Let
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{2n\times 2n}

be the error in the invertibility of the frame

{\hat{P}}
under the assumption that
{\hat{P}}
is symplectic. That is,

(4.47)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}:={\hat{P}}\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)-I_{2n}.

In what follows, we will need certain matrix to be sufficiently
close to the identity. In particular, we will need
I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}

to be invertible in
{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}_{\rho-2\delta}
. This can be
ensured if

(4.48)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\hat{\nu}<1.

For instance, if (
4.48
) holds, then
{\hat{P}}^{-1}
is
invertible with inverse

{\hat{P}}^{-1}=-\left(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\right)^{-1}{\Omega_{0}}{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}.

In the remaining of this section, we will assume
(
4.48
) holds.

Lemma 4.6
.

The error in the invertibility of
{\hat{P}}
is given by

(4.49)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}={\hat{P}}{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\left(I-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\right)^{-1}{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}.

Additionally, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)

(4.50)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

Note that

-{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}=I-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle{\hat{P}}\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)

\displaystyle={\hat{P}}\big(I-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\big){\hat{P}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=I+{\hat{P}}{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\left(I-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\right)^{-1}{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}

and

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}={\hat{P}}{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\left(I-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\right)^{-1}{\Omega_{0}}{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}.

For estimate (
4.50
), since

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\hat{\nu}<1
by assumption,

\left\|{\left(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\right)^{-1}}\right\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\right\|_{\rho-2\delta}^{k}\leq\frac{1}{1-\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\hat{\nu}}.

Then, from (
4.49
) and Lemma
4.4
, we
obtain

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\hat{\nu}}C_{P}C_{{P}^{\!\top\!}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

∎

4.8.
Approximate symmetries of
\Lambda^{-1}\hat{S}
and

{A}

The use of the matrix-valued map
{A}
to reduce the torsion is
equivalent to applying the following transformation to the frame
{\hat{P}}

(4.51)

{P}={\hat{P}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}I_{n}&{A}\\
\hline\cr&I_{n}\end{array}\right).

This transformation is symplectic—and consequently so is

{P}
—if
{A}
is symmetric. Recall
that according to remark
3.1
,
{A}
is symmetric if
the symmetry condition (
3.11
) holds, which only
holds approximately when
K
and
{W}
satisfy (
3.1
) and
(
3.2
) approximately. In order to control the
approximate geometric properties of the frame
{P}
, we need to
control the error in the symmetry of
{A}
which in turn is
controlled by the error in
the symmetry condition (
3.11
).
Let us define the following errors in the symmetry of

\Lambda^{-1}\hat{S}
and
{A}

{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}},E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n}
be defined as

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}}

\displaystyle=\Lambda^{-1}\hat{S}-\big(\Lambda^{-1}\hat{S}\big)^{{\!\top\!}},

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}

\displaystyle={A}-{A}^{\!\top\!}.

Lemma 4.7
.

For any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)
the torsion
\hat{S}
satisfies

(4.52)

\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

Let us inspect the symplecticity of the reduced dynamics
under the frame
{\hat{P}}
with respect to

{\Omega_{0}}
, i.e., the symplecticity of the following matrix-valued
map

{\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}:=\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}\Lambda&\hat{S}\\
\hline\cr&\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{$\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}$}\end{array}\right).

A straightforward computation reveals

(4.53)

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm symp}${\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}$}}:

\displaystyle={\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega_{0}}{\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}-{\Omega_{0}}

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}&\\
\hline\cr\rule{0.0pt}{11.99998pt}&\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{$\Lambda^{-1}\hat{S}-\big(\Lambda^{-1}\hat{S}\big)^{{\!\top\!}}$}\end{array}\right).

Then, from (
4.42
) and (
4.47
)
we obtain

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm symp}${\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}$}}=

\displaystyle-{\Omega_{0}}-{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega_{0}}{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\hat{P}}

\displaystyle-{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}-E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega_{0}}{\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\Omega_{0}}+{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}\big({\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})}\mspace{1.5mu}-{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}\big)\big(I+E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big){\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\hat{P}}

\displaystyle-{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}-E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega_{0}}{\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}},

and using the symplecticity of
\phi_{T}
and (
4.39
), we
have

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm symp}${\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}$}}=

\displaystyle E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}-E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega_{0}}{\Lambda}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\hat{P}}$}}-{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\hat{P}}

\displaystyle+{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\hat{P}}

\displaystyle-{{\hat{P}}^{\!\top\!}}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}.

Lastly, from (
4.53
) and lemmas

4.4
,
4.5
and

4.6
, we obtain

(4.56)

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}}=

\displaystyle{B^{\!\top\!}}{\Omega_{L}}{B}-E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}^{22^{\!\top\!}}\hat{S}-\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Delta\Omega\hskip 0.86108pt}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle+\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm inv}$\scriptstyle{\hat{P}}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\hat{N}

\displaystyle-\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}\big({\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})\big)^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\hat{P}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}^{22}

from where estimate (
4.52
) follows.
∎

Lemma 4.8
.

Let
{A}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n}

satisfy (
3.14
)-(
3.16
), where

{A}=\begin{pmatrix}{\mathcal{O}}_{n-1}&A_{2}\\
A_{3}&A_{4}\end{pmatrix}.

Then, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)
,
{A}
satisfies

(4.57)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}$}}^{W}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

First, notice that

{A}-{A}^{\!\top\!}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}&A_{2}-A_{3}^{\!\top\!}\\
\hline\cr\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{$A_{3}-A_{2}^{\!\top\!}$}&\end{array}\right).

If we multiply (
3.14
) by
\lambda
and subtract
the transpose of
(
3.15
)
, we obtain

{\mathfrak{L}}^{-\lambda 1}\left(A_{2}-A_{3}^{\!\top\!}\right)=\hat{S}_{2}\lambda-\hat{S}_{3}^{\!\top\!}.

Similarly, we can multiply the transpose of (
3.14
) by

\lambda
and subtract it to (
3.15
) to obtain

{\mathfrak{L}}^{-\lambda 1}\left(A_{3}-A_{2}^{\!\top\!}\right)=\hat{S}_{3}-\lambda\hat{S}_{2}^{\!\top\!}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\|{A_{2}-A_{3}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-|{\lambda}|}\|{\hat{S}_{2}\lambda-\hat{S}_{3}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{1}{1-|{\lambda}|}\|{\hat{S}\Lambda^{\!\top\!}-\Lambda\hat{S}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},

\displaystyle\|{A_{3}-A_{2}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-|{\lambda}|}\|{\hat{S}_{3}-\lambda\hat{S}_{2}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{1}{1-|{\lambda}|}\|{\hat{S}\Lambda^{\!\top\!}-\Lambda\hat{S}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},

from where we obtain the estimate

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{1}{1-|{\lambda}|}\|{\hat{S}\Lambda^{\!\top\!}-\Lambda\hat{S}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}$\Lambda^{\!-1}\!\hat{S}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},

where we used that
\|{\Lambda}\|
=1.
We can now use Lemma
4.7
and obtain
(
4.57
).

∎

4.9.
Approximate symplecticity of the frame

{P}

Let
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{2n\times 2n}
denote the error in the
symplecticity of
{P}
. That is,

(4.58)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}:={P}^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K}\mspace{1.5mu}{P}-{\Omega_{0}}.

Lemma 4.9
.

For any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)
the symplecticity error
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}

satisfies

(4.59)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

From definition (
4.58
) and transformation
(
4.51
) we obtain

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}I_{n}&\\
\hline\cr\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{${A^{\!\top\!}}$}&I_{n}\end{array}\right)E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}I_{n}&{A}\\
\hline\cr&I_{n}\end{array}\right)+\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}&\\
\hline\cr&\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{${A}-{A^{\!\top\!}}$}\end{array}\right).

Consequently,

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\|{{A^{\!\top\!}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\left(1+\|{{A}}\|_{\rho}\right)\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+{C_{{A^{\!\top\!}}}}\right)\left(1+{C_{{A}}}\right)\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{{\rm sym}${A}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}.

Then, using Lemmas
4.4
and
4.8
,
result (
4.59
) is a direct computation.
∎

4.10.
Approximate reducibility of the frame
{P}

Let us now define the error in the reducibility of the linearized
dynamics in the coordinates given by the frame
{P}
as

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{2n\times 2n}
given by

(4.60)

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}:=-{\Omega_{0}}{P}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){P}-\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}\Lambda&S\\
\hline\cr&\raisebox{0.0pt}[9.90276pt][0.0pt]{$\Lambda^{-{\!\top\!}}$}\end{array}\right).

Lemma 4.10
.

For any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)
the reducibility error
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}

satisfies

(4.61)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

As in the proof of Lemma
4.9
, we use definition
(
4.60
) and transformation (
4.51
) to
obtain

E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}I_{n}&-{A^{\!\top\!}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\\
\hline\cr&I_{n}\end{array}\right)E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c|c}I_{n}&{A}\\
\hline\cr&I_{n}\end{array}\right),

where we used that
{A}
solves (
3.8
).
Then,

\displaystyle\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\|{{A^{\!\top\!}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\left(1+\|{{A}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\right)\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\left(1+{C_{{A^{\!\top\!}}}}\right)\left(1+{C_{{A}}}\right)\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},

from where we obtain (
4.61
).
∎

5.
Torus and bundle corrections

In this section, we provide two lemmas to control the error in
the torus and bundle parameterizations after one step of the
iterative scheme. The lemmas rely on the bounds for the
approximate geometric properties obtained in Section

4
.

5.1.
The linearized equation for the torus

Let us now inspect the new error in the invariance equation after the
iterative step of Section
3.2
, i.e., after choosing
\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}
that solves
(
3.23
)-(
3.26
). A straightforward calculation reveals

(5.1)

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}:=

\displaystyle\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K+{\Delta K},\mathrm{id})-K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}-{\Delta K}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}+{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\Delta K}-\Delta K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}+{\Delta^{2}\phi_{T}},

where

{\Delta^{2}\phi_{T}}:=\int_{0}^{1}(1-s){\rm D}^{2}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K+s{\Delta K},\mathrm{id})\left[{\Delta K},{\Delta K}\right]ds

is only well-defined in
{\tilde{\mathcal{U}}}
for sufficiently small
{\Delta K}
.
Similarly as in Section
4.2
, we can guarantee so if

(5.2)

\frac{\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}}{R-\|{K-K_{0}}\|_{\rho}}<1.

Also, we will need
I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}
to be invertible in

{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}_{\rho-2\delta}
. This condition is granted if

(5.3)

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\nu<1

holds—recall the analogous assumption (
4.48
) in
Section
4.7
. For the remaining of this section, we
incorporate (
5.2
) and (
5.3
) to our
assumptions.

Lemma 5.1
.

The new error in the torus invariance equation after correcting

K
by solving
(
3.22
) is given by

(5.4)

{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}={P}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big)^{-1}E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}+{\Delta^{2}\phi_{T}},

where

E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}:=E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}+{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}-\begin{pmatrix}0_{n\times 1}\\
{\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}\\
0_{1\times 1}\end{pmatrix}.

Additionally, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/2)

(5.5)

\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}^{{KK}}}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}^{{K{W}}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

Let us begin by inspecting the sum

{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}+{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\Delta K}-{\Delta K}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}.

If we left-multiply it by
-{\Omega_{0}}{P}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}
and by
its inverse, we obtain

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}+{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\Delta K}-{\Delta K}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}

\displaystyle=\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{P}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)^{-1}\Big(-\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}+{\Lambda_{\scalebox{0.65}{${P}$}}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}+E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}-\big(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big)\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big)

\displaystyle=\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{P}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)^{-1}\left(E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}+{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}-\begin{pmatrix}0_{n\times 1}\\
{\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}\\
0_{1\times 1}\end{pmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{P}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)^{-1}E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}},

where we used definitions (
4.58
), (
4.60
),
and that
\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}
satisfies (
3.23
)-(
3.26
).
Recall that by assumption
\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\nu<1
so

I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}
is invertible.
Then, from the previous expression, that

(5.6)

\Big(-{\Omega_{0}}P^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big)^{-1}=P{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\Big)^{-1},

and (
5.1
), we obtain (
5.4
).
For estimate (
5.5
), we first
obtain estimates for each
\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{i}
as

(5.7)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{1}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{{N}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{1}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.8)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{2}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{{N}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{2}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.9)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq{\max{\left\{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}},\ {C_{{{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}}}}\right\}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{3}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.10)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{4}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{4}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

and for each
\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{i}
we obtain the following estimates from
(
3.27
)-(
3.31
)

(5.11)

\displaystyle|{{\langle{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}}|\leq\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{1}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}+{C_{\hat{S}}}c_{\mathfrak{R}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{3}$}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\xi_{K}^{3}}\rangle}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.12)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{1}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq c_{\mathfrak{R}}\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{1}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}+{C_{\hat{S}}}\left(c_{\mathfrak{R}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{3}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\xi_{K}^{3}}\rangle}$}}}\right)\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{1}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.13)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{2}}\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{2}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{2}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.14)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\|_{\rho-\delta}\leq\left(c_{\mathfrak{R}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{3}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\xi_{K}^{3}}\rangle}$}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{3}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.15)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{4}}\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{K}^{4}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{4}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Therefore,

(5.16)

\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq{\max{\left\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{1}$}}},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{2}$}}}\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{3}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{4}$}}}\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}\right\}}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

and, for
{\Delta K}=P\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}
, we have the estimate

(5.17)

\displaystyle\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\Big({C_{{L}}}{\max{\left\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{1}$}}},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{2}$}}}\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}\right\}}}+{C_{{N}}}{\max{\left\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{3}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}^{4}$}}}\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}\right\}}}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

In order to obtain estimates for
|{{\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}}|
,
let

\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}=\begin{pmatrix}({\rm D}_{\theta}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu})^{\!\top\!}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\\
{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\end{pmatrix}=:\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{31}\\
\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{32}\end{pmatrix}.

We now make use of the following lemma from
[
FMHM24
]
,

Lemma 5.2
.

The averages of
\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{31}
and
\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{32}
are

\displaystyle\langle\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{31}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle({\rm D}_{\theta}{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}})^{\!\top\!}\Delta^{1}a+({\rm D}_{\theta}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu})^{\!\top\!}\Delta^{2}a\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{32}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\Delta^{2}H\rangle-\langle(\Delta^{1}a)^{\!\top\!}{\rm D}_{\varphi}{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\hat{\alpha}\rangle-\langle(\Delta^{2}a)^{\!\top\!}{\rm D}_{\varphi}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\hat{\alpha}\rangle,

where the Taylor remainders
\Delta^{i}
of order
i
in
E^{K}
are given by

\displaystyle\Delta^{1}a:=

\displaystyle a{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})-a{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}{\rm D}a{\!\>\circ\!\>}\big(K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}+s{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\big){E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\ ds,

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}a:=

\displaystyle a{\!\>\circ\!\>}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id})-a{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}-{\rm D}a{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}(1-s){\rm D}^{2}a{\!\>\circ\!\>}\big(K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}+s{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}\big)[{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}},{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}]\ ds,

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}H:=

\displaystyle H{\!\>\circ\!\>}\big(\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}),{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big)-H{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu},{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big)-{\rm D}_{z}H{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu},{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}){E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}(1-s){\rm D}^{2}_{z}H{\!\>\circ\!\>}\big(K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}+s{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}},{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big)[{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}},{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}]\ ds.

Since—by assumption—the integral Taylor remainders are well
defined, we can obtain the estimates

(5.18)

\displaystyle|{{\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{31}}\rangle}}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(2nc_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}a}$}}+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}^{2}\!a}$}}\frac{\delta}{2}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{31}}\rangle}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2},

(5.19)

\displaystyle|{{\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{32}}\rangle}}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(2n\ell c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$({{\rm D}a})^{\!\top\!}$}}|{\hat{\alpha}}|+({c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\rm D}^{2}_{z}H$}}}+2n\ell c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}^{2}\!a}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}}|{\hat{\alpha}}|)\frac{\delta}{2}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{32}}\rangle}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2},

and

(5.20)

|{{\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}}|\leq{\max{\left\{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{31}}\rangle}$}}},\ {C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{32}}\rangle}$}}}\right\}}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}$}}}}{\delta}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}.

To control
E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}
, we will use Lemma
4.9
which
applies if
\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\hat{\nu}<1
; which recall is
one of our assumptions. Therefore, using
Lemmas
4.9
and
4.10
, we obtain

(5.21)

\displaystyle\|{E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{K}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}\right){C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\langle{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}^{3}}\rangle}$}}}\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau+1}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\right){C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{K}$}}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K{W}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Observe that using Neumann series,

(5.22)

\|{\left(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}\right)^{-1}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{1}{1-\nu},

Also, note that
{\Delta^{2}\phi_{T}}
is well-defined by assumption
(
5.2
). Hence,

\|{{\Delta^{2}\phi_{T}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\frac{1}{2}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\|{\Delta K}\|_{\rho-2\delta}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}(C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}})^{2}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|^{2}_{\rho}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau}}.

We now have all the estimates to obtain

(5.23)

\displaystyle\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}\left(\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K{W}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)+\frac{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}(C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}})^{2}}{2\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}+\frac{1}{2}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}^{2}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau}}+\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{K}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K{W}}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}^{{KK}}}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}^{{K{W}}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

∎

5.2.
The linearized equation for the bundle

Let us now inspect the new error in the bundle invariance equation.
First recall definition (
3.33
). The new
error in the bundle invariance equation is the following

(5.24)

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}:=

\displaystyle{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K+{\Delta K},\mathrm{id})({W}+{\Delta{W}})-\big({W}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}+{\Delta{W}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big)(\lambda+{\Delta\lambda})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}+{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\Delta{W}}-{\Delta{W}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\lambda-{W}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\lambda}

\displaystyle+{\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]-{\Delta{W}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\lambda},

where, for any
\zeta:{\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{2n}
,

{\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[\zeta]=\int_{0}^{1}{{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K+s{\Delta K},\mathrm{id})[{\Delta K},\zeta]ds.

Lemma 5.3
.

The new error in the bundle invariance
equation after correcting
W
by solving (
3.35
)
is given by

(5.25)

{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}={P}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big)^{-1}E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}+{\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]-{\Delta{W}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\lambda},

where

E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}:=E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}+{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big(\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\lambda+e_{n}{\Delta\lambda}\big)

Additionally, for any
\delta\in(0,\rho/3)

(5.26)

\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{KK}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{{W}{W}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{K{W}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Proof.

Let us first inspect the sum

\displaystyle{\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}+{\rm D}_{z}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(K,\mathrm{id}){\Delta{W}}-{\Delta{W}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\lambda-{W}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Delta\lambda}.

If left-multiply it by
-{\Omega_{0}}{P^{\!\top\!}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}
and by its inverse,
we obtain

\displaystyle\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{P^{\!\top\!}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)^{-1}\Big(-\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}+{\Lambda_{\scalebox{0.65}{${P}$}}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}+E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}

\displaystyle-\left(I_{2n}-{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)\left(\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\lambda+e_{n}{\Delta\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{P^{\!\top\!}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)^{-1}\bigg(E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}+{\Omega_{0}}E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\big(\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\lambda+e_{n}{\Delta\lambda}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(-{\Omega_{0}}{P^{\!\top\!}}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}{\Omega{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}}\mspace{1.5mu}\right)^{-1}E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}},

where we used definitions (
4.58
), (
4.60
),
and that
\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}
satisfies (
3.35
).
Result (
5.25
) then follows from using this last
expression in (
5.24
), the assumption

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}i\leq\nu<1
, and (
5.6
).
In order to obtain estimate (

), note that

{\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}
is well-defined by assumption (
5.2
)
and that

(5.27)

\|{{\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}+\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{{C_{{\scalebox{0.75}{${\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}$}}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{{\scalebox{0.75}{${\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}$}}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Then, we proceed to obtain estimates for each

as

(5.28)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{1}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{{N}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\left(\frac{{C_{{\scalebox{0.75}{${\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}$}}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{1}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{1}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.29)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{2}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{{N}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\left(\frac{{C_{{\scalebox{0.75}{${\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}$}}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

(5.30)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{3}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{\max{\left\{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}},\ {C_{{{\mathcal{X}}^{\!\top\!}}}}\right\}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\left(\frac{{C_{{\scalebox{0.75}{${\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}$}}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{3}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{3}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.31)

\displaystyle\|{\eta_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{4}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}\left(\frac{{C_{{\scalebox{0.75}{${\widetilde{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}$}}}^{K}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

and for each

)-(

(5.32)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{1}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.33)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{2}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{\mathfrak{R}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{2}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.34)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{3}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}}\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{3}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{3}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.35)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}^{4}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}
