\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

Then,

(5.36)

\displaystyle\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\max\Big\{

\displaystyle\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},\ \frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

\displaystyle\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},\ \frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip-34.1667pt\leq\max\Big\{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}},\ {C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Big\}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\hskip-23.61119pt+\max\Big\{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}},\ {C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Big\}\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\hskip-34.1667pt\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

We can then control the correction of the bundle parameterization and
the rate of contraction as

(5.37)

\displaystyle\|{{\Delta{W}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{L}}}{\max{\left\{\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},\ \frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right\}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+{C_{{N}}}{\max{\left\{\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},\ \frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right\}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left({C_{{L}}}{\max{\left\{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}\right\}}}+{C_{{N}}}{\max{\left\{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}},\ {C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\right\}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left({C_{{L}}}{\max{\left\{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{1}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ {C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{2}$}}}\right\}}}+{C_{{N}}}{\max{\left\{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{3}$}}},\ {C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{W}^{4}$}}}\right\}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.38)

\displaystyle|{{\Delta\lambda}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{2}$}}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\eta_{W}^{2}$}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}.

In order to control
E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}
, we will use Lemmas
4.9

and
4.10
. Recall that Lemma
4.9

applies from the assumption
\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|\leq\hat{\nu}<1
.
Therefore, we can proceed as follows

(5.39)

\displaystyle\|{E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}+\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\|{\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}|{\lambda}|+\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}|{{\Delta\lambda}}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left(\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\left(\frac{{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left(\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\left(\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{K}}{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau+1}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\Big({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}+\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm red${P}$}}$}}^{K}}{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\xi_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}$}}}\right)\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau-1}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{KK}}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K{W}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{{W}{W}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}.

Lastly, we can control the remaining terms in (
5.25
) as

(5.40)

\displaystyle\|{\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}[{\Delta{W}}]}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\left(\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C^{KK}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]$}}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C^{K{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho},

(5.41)

\displaystyle\|{{\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}+C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{KK}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{C^{K{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}.

We can now use the assumption

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\nu<1

and (
5.22
) to finally obtain the estimate
(
5.26
) as

(5.42)

\displaystyle\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}\left(\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{KK}}}{\gamma^{4}\delta^{4\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K{W}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{{W}{W}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\frac{{C^{KK}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]$}}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C^{K{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]$}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\frac{C^{KK}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{C^{K{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{KK}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}+{C^{KK}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]$}}}+C^{KK}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left(\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{K{W}}}+{C^{K{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}}[{\Delta{W}}]$}}}+C^{K{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\left(\frac{C_{P}}{1-\nu}{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E^{W}_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm lin}}$}}^{{W}{W}}}+C^{{W}{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}{\Delta\lambda}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{KK}}}{\gamma^{5}\delta^{5\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{K{W}}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{{W}{W}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}^{2}.

∎

6.
Proof of the KAM theorem

As mentioned previously, the iterative scheme described in
Section
3.2
allows the construction of a
sequence of objects. The key idea for the proof of Theorem
3.4

consists in showing that the sequence for
K,{W},
and
\lambda

converge—under certain conditions—to solutions of
(
3.1
) and (
3.2
). First, we obtain an
iterative lemma and derive conditions such that, if satisfied, the
iterative lemma can be applied again to the objects obtained
after one step of the Quasi-Newton method. Convergence then
follows from ensuring that the conditions necessary for the
iterative lemma are satisfied at every step of the iterative
process.

6.1.
The iterative lemma

After one step of the iterative scheme, we obtain the new
parameterizations

\bar{K}\leftarrow K+{\Delta K}
and
\bar{W}\leftarrow{W}+{\Delta{W}}
and
the new rate of contraction
\bar{\lambda}\leftarrow\lambda+{\Delta\lambda}
. From

\bar{K},\bar{W}
, and
\bar{\lambda}
,
we obtain the new objects
\hat{\bar{N}},\bar{B},
and
\bar{S}
.
The following lemma allows the control of the new objects—and
consequently the control of the iterative procedure.

Lemma 6.1

(The iterative lemma)
.

Assume the hypothesis of Theorem
3.4
.
For every
\delta\in(0,\rho/3)
, there exists a constant
{C_{\Delta}}
such that
if

(6.1)

\frac{{C_{\Delta}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}<1

holds, where

{\mathcal{E}}={\max{\left\{\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}},\ \|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right\}}},

(6.2)

\displaystyle{C_{\Delta}}:=\max\Bigg\{

\displaystyle{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ \frac{{\max{\left\{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau},C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\right\}}}}{R-\|{K-{K_{0}}}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ \frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}-\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},

\displaystyle\frac{\ell C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}}-\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}K}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},\ \frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}-\|{({{\rm D}_{\theta}K})^{{\!\top\!}}}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},

\displaystyle\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\varphi}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}-\|{({{\rm D}_{\varphi}K})^{{\!\top\!}}}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},\ \frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}-\|{{W}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}-\|{{W^{\!\top\!}}}\|_{\rho}},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}}{{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}-\|{{B}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}-\|{\hat{N}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}-\|{\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}-\big|{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}\big|},

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}-|{\lambda}|}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ \frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}-|{\lambda^{\!-1}}|}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Bigg\},

then we have new parameterizations

\bar{K}\in\left({\mathcal{A}}\big({\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}_{\rho-2\delta}\big)\right)^{2n}
,

\bar{W}\in\left({\mathcal{A}}\big({\mathbb{T}}^{d+\ell}_{\rho-3\delta}\big)\right)^{2n}

and their associated objects satisfy

(6.3)

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}},

(6.4)

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}},

(6.5)

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}},

(6.6)

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\varphi}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}},

(6.7)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{W}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}},

(6.8)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{W}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}},

(6.9)

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{N}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}},

(6.10)

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{N}}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}},

(6.11)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{B}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}<{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}},

(6.12)

\displaystyle\left|{{\langle{\bar{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{-1}}\right|<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}.

Furthermore, the hyperbolicity is controlled by

(6.13)

|{\bar{\lambda}}|<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}},\quad|{{\bar{\lambda}}^{-1}}|<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}},

and the new objects are close to the original ones in the
following sense

(6.14)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{K}-K}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.15)

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K}-{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.16)

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K}-{\rm D}_{\varphi}K}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{\ell C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.17)

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}-({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.18)

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}-({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.19)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{W}-{W}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.20)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{W}^{\!\top\!}-{W^{\!\top\!}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.21)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{B}-{B}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.22)

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{N}}-\hat{N}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.23)

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{N}}^{\!\top\!}-\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.24)

\displaystyle\left|{{\langle{\bar{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}-{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}}\right|\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.25)

\displaystyle|{\bar{\lambda}-\lambda}|\leq C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.26)

\displaystyle|{{\bar{\lambda}}^{-1}-\lambda^{\!-1}}|\leq C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}$}}{\mathcal{E}}.

Lastly, the new errors in the invariance equations

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}

\displaystyle=\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(\bar{K},\mathrm{id})-\bar{K}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu},

\displaystyle{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}

\displaystyle={{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}{\!\>\circ\!\>}(\bar{K},\mathrm{id})\bar{W}-\bar{W}{\!\>\circ\!\>}{{\mathcal{R}}}_{\omega\alpha}\mspace{1.5mu}\bar{\lambda},

satisfy

(6.27)

\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}}^{2},\quad\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}^{2}.

Proof.

Recall that in order to use Lemmas
5.1
and

5.3
, we need hypothesis (
4.48
),
(
5.3
), (
4.12
), and (
5.2
)
to hold. Let us take for the values of
\hat{\nu}
and
\nu
the
following

(6.28)

\displaystyle\hat{\nu}

\displaystyle=\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\right){\mathcal{E}}={C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.29)

\displaystyle\nu

\displaystyle=\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{K}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}^{{W}}}\right){\mathcal{E}}={C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}},

which clearly satisfy
\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${\hat{P}}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\hat{\nu}
and

\|{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\nu
. It is not hard to show that

\hat{\nu}\leq\nu
; see Table
3
. Then,
from hypothesis (
6.1
) for the first term in
(
6.2
) we conclude that (
4.48
) and
(
5.3
) are satisfied.
For (
4.12
) and (
5.2
), observe that by hypothesis
(
6.1
) for the second term in
(
6.2
)

\frac{{\max{\left\{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau},C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\right\}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{R-\|{K-{K_{0}}}\|_{\rho}}\frac{{\mathcal{E}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}<1.

Then, from the definition of
{\mathcal{E}}
and estimate (
5.17
), we conclude that
(
4.12
) and (
5.2
) are also satisfied.
Furthermore, estimates (
6.14
), (
6.19
), and
(
6.20
) are
immediate from (
5.17
) and (
5.37
), respectively.
For estimates (
6.3
)-(
6.6
), we
proceed as follows

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}+\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}+\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}K}\|_{\rho}+\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}K}\|_{\rho}+\frac{\ell C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}{\Delta K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}{\Delta K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\leq\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau+1}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho},

where we used Cauchy estimates on
{\Delta K}
. Note that these Cauchy
estimates result directly in
(
6.15
)-(
6.18
). Then,

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}+\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}K}\|_{\rho}+\frac{\ell C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}{\mathcal{E}},

and by hypothesis (
6.1
)—in particular for the third
to sixth terms in (
6.2
)—we conclude

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}},

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}},

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}},

\displaystyle\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}\bar{K})^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\varphi}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}.

Similarly, for the corrected parameterization of the bundle,
from estimate (
5.37
) we have

(6.30)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{W}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{W}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{W}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle\|{\bar{W}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{W}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{W}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}.

By hypothesis (
6.1
) for the seventh and
eighth terms in (
6.2
), we obtain estimates
(
6.7
) and (
6.8
).
In order to control
\bar{L}
, note that

\Delta{L}:=\bar{L}-{L}=\begin{pmatrix}{\rm D}_{\theta}{\Delta K}\ \Big|\ \widetilde{\Delta X}-{\rm D}_{\varphi}{\Delta K}\hat{\alpha}\ \Big|\ {\Delta{W}}\end{pmatrix},

where

\widetilde{\Delta X}:=\int_{0}^{1}{\rm D}_{z}X{\!\>\circ\!\>}\left(K+s{\Delta K},\mathrm{id}\right){\Delta K}ds,

which is well-defined in
{\mathbb{T}}^{d}_{\rho-2\delta}\times{\mathbb{T}}^{\ell}_{\rho-2\delta}

by hypothesis (
6.1
) for the second term in
(
6.2
). Then, using (
5.17
),
(
5.37
), and Cauchy estimates, we obtain

(6.31)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{L}-{L}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left(d+\ell|{\hat{\alpha}}|+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\rm D}_{z}X$}}\delta\right)C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}+C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}+\frac{C^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.32)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{L}^{\!\top\!}-{L}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\max\Bigg\{\frac{2n}{\delta}\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},\ 2n\left(c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$({\rm D}_{z}X)^{\!\top\!}$}}+\frac{|{\hat{\alpha}^{\!\top\!}}|}{\delta}\right)\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta},\ 2n\|{{\Delta{W}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2n}{\delta}\max\Big\{1,c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$({\rm D}_{z}X)^{\!\top\!}$}}\delta+|{\hat{\alpha}^{\!\top\!}}|\Big\}\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+2n\|{{\Delta{W}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 2n\left(\max\Big\{1,c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$({\rm D}_{z}X)^{\!\top\!}$}}\delta+|{\hat{\alpha}^{\!\top\!}}|\Big\}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1}+C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}+\frac{2nC^{W}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}.

Let us now control
\Delta{B}:=\bar{B}-{B}
. First,
for the restricted metric, we have that

G_{\bar{L}}-G_{L}={L}^{\!\top\!}\Delta G\bar{L}+{L}^{\!\top\!}G{\!\>\circ\!\>}K\Delta{L}+\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}G{\!\>\circ\!\>}\bar{K}\bar{L},

where

\Delta G:=G{\!\>\circ\!\>}\bar{K}-G{\!\>\circ\!\>}K=\int_{0}^{1}{\rm D}G\left(K+s{\Delta K}\right){\Delta K}ds,

which is well-defined in

{\mathbb{T}}^{d}_{\rho-2\delta}\times{\mathbb{T}}^{\ell}_{\rho-2\delta}
by hypothesis
(
6.1
) for the second term in (
6.2
).
Then,

(6.33)

\displaystyle\|{G_{\bar{L}}-G_{L}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\rm D}G$}}{C_{{L}}}\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}+{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$G$}}}\|{\Delta{L}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}+{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$G$}}}{C_{{L}}}\|{\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left({C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\rm D}G$}}{C_{{L}}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}+{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$G$}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}+{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$G$}}}{C_{{L}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\left({C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$G$}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}+{c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$G$}}}{C_{{L}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta G_{L}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta G_{L}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta G_{L}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}.

We will now use the following result that is presented for matrices
but extends to matrix valued maps
[
FH24
]
.

Lemma 6.2
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n\times n}
be an invertible matrix satisfying

|M^{-1}|<\sigma
and assume that
\bar{M}\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n\times n}
satisfies

\frac{\sigma^{2}|\bar{M}-M|}{\sigma-|M^{-1}|}<1.

Then,
\bar{M}
is invertible and

|\bar{M}^{-1}|<\sigma,\quad|\bar{M}^{-1}-M^{-1}|\leq\sigma^{2}|\bar{M}-M|.

Note that by hypothesis (
6.1
) for the ninth term in
(
6.2
),

\frac{({\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}})^{2}\|{G_{\bar{L}}-G_{L}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}}{{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}-\|{B}\|_{\rho}}<1.

Therefore, applying Lemma
6.2
to
G_{\bar{L}}({\theta,\varphi})
and

G_{L}({\theta,\varphi})
, we obtain (
6.21
) and (
6.11
)
as

(6.34)

\displaystyle\|{\bar{B}-{B}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq({\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}})^{2}\|{G_{\bar{L}}-G_{L}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{({\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}})^{2}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta G_{L}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{({\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}})^{2}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta G_{L}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle\|{\bar{B}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle<{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}.

Let us proceed and control
\Delta\hat{N}:=\hat{\bar{N}}-\hat{N}
and

\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}:=\hat{\bar{N}}^{\!\top\!}-\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}
. Note that

\hat{\bar{N}}-\hat{N}={J}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K{L}\Delta{B}+{J}{\!\>\circ\!\>}K\Delta{L}\bar{B}+\Delta{J}\bar{L}\bar{B},

{\mathbb{T}}^{d}_{\rho-2\delta}\times{\mathbb{T}}^{\ell}_{\rho-2\delta}

6.1
) for the second term in
(
6.2
). Then, we obtain
(
6.22
) and (
6.23
) as

(6.35)

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{N}}-\hat{N}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}$}}{C_{{L}}}\|{\Delta{B}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}$}}\|{\Delta{L}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\rm D}{J}$}}\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}{C_{{L}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}$}}\left({C_{{L}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}+C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}\right)+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\rm D}{J}$}}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}{C_{{L}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}$}}\left({C_{{L}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}+C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}$}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

(6.36)

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{N}}^{\!\top\!}-\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}^{\!\top\!}$}}\|{\Delta{B}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}+{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}\|{\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}^{\!\top\!}$}}+{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${(\rm D}{J})^{\!\top\!}$}}2n\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}^{\!\top\!}$}}\left({C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}+{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}\right)+{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}{C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${(\rm D}{J})^{\!\top\!}$}}2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${J}^{\!\top\!}$}}\left({C_{{{L}}^{\!\top\!}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}}+{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{L}^{\!\top\!}$}}\right)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}.

Also, by hypothesis (

) for the tenth and

Note that, according to (

Similarly as for

\displaystyle\|{\hat{\bar{S}}-\hat{S}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\|{\Delta\hat{N}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\left(c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\right)\|{{\Delta K}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+\|{\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\left(c_{\scalebox{0.65}{${{\rm D}\Omega}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}+c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}^{2}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\right)C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}\gamma\delta^{\tau}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq}+C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}+\Big(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}+C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Omega$}}c_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${{\rm D}_{z}}\phi_{T}$}}}\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}\Big)\frac{\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{S}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{S}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{S}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}.

In order to obtain estimates (
6.12
) and

), we need to control the reduced torsion
{\mathcal{S}}
.
We will use Lemma

which
holds if

Observe that

\left|{{\langle{\bar{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}-{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}}\right|\leq\left|{{\langle{\hat{\bar{S}}}\rangle}-{\langle{\hat{S}}\rangle}}\right|\leq\|{\hat{\bar{S}}-\hat{S}}\|_{\rho-3\delta},

Therefore, (

is invertible, and

\displaystyle\left|{{\langle{\bar{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}-{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}\right)^{2}\|{\hat{\bar{S}}-\hat{S}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}\right)^{2}C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{S}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{\left(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}\right)^{2}C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{S}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}}{\gamma^{3}\delta^{3\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+\frac{C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle\left|{{\langle{\bar{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}}\right|

\displaystyle<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}.

For the new rate of contraction, observe that

|{\bar{\lambda}}|\leq|{\lambda}|+|{{\Delta\lambda}}|,\quad|{{\bar{\lambda}}^{-1}}|\leq|{\lambda^{\!-1}}|+|{{\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}}|.

Therefore, the hyperbolicity controls (
6.13
) hold if

\frac{|{{\Delta\lambda}}|}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}-|{\lambda}|}<1,\quad\frac{|{{\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}}|}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}-|{\lambda^{\!-1}}|}<1.

Note that from bound (

) and hypothesis
(
6.1
) for the thirteenth term in (

)

(6.40)

\frac{|{{\Delta\lambda}}|}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}-|{\lambda}|}\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}-|{\lambda}|}\left(\frac{C^{K}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$K$}}}}\|_{\rho}+C^{{W}}_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{${W}$}}}}\|_{\rho}\right)\leq\frac{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}-|{\lambda}|}{\mathcal{E}}<1.

Also, hypothesis (

) for the last term in
(
6.2
) allow us to use Lemma

and
obtain

(6.41)

\displaystyle|{{\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}}|

\displaystyle\leq(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}})^{2}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}{\mathcal{E}},

\displaystyle|{{\bar{\lambda}}^{-1}}|

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}.

For estimates (
6.27
) we will use Lemmas

5.1
and
5.3
. From
(

) we have that

(6.42)

\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{K}$}}}}\|_{\rho-2\delta}\leq\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}^{{KK}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}^{{K{W}}}}\right){\mathcal{E}}^{2}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{K}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}}^{2}.

Also, from (
5.26
), we obtain

(6.43)

\displaystyle\|{{E_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\bar{W}$}}}}\|_{\rho-3\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left({C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{KK}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{{W}{W}}}+{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}^{K{W}}}\right)\frac{{\mathcal{E}}^{2}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\bar{W}}$}}}}{\gamma\delta^{\tau}}{\mathcal{E}}^{2}.

∎

6.2.
Convergence of the iterative procedure and proof of the
KAM theorem

We show that under the assumptions of Theorem
3.4
,
condition (
6.1
) in Lemma
6.1
holds
at every step of the iterative scheme. Therefore, Lemma
6.1

can be applied infinitely many times. In the
limit, the sequences of objects converge and define invariant
tori and its invariant bundles.

Consider the objects at the iterative step
j
denoted by
K_{j},~{W}_{j},~\lambda_{j},~\hat{N}_{j},~{B}_{j},
and
{\mathcal{S}}_{j}
. Consider also the
errors
E_{K_{j}}
and
E_{{W}_{j}}
in the invariance
equations (
3.19
) (
3.20
), respectively,
the analyticity domain
\rho_{j}
, and analyticity bite
\delta_{j}
. For
the step
j=0
, consider the objects declared in theorem

3.4
. That is, take
K_{0}=K,~{W}_{0}={W},~\lambda_{0}=\lambda,~\hat{N}_{0}=\hat{N},~{B}_{0}={B},~{\mathcal{S}}_{0}={\mathcal{S}},~\rho_{0}=\rho,~\delta_{0}=\delta
, and

{\mathcal{E}}_{0}={\mathcal{E}}
.
Note that the domain of analyticity of the objects decreases at
every step according to

\rho_{j}=\rho_{j-1}-3\delta_{j-1},

where
\delta_{j}
is the domain bite chosen for step
j
. Let us then
choose the following geometric sequence of bites

\delta_{j}=\frac{\delta}{a^{j}},

for some
a>1
—which results in the limiting width

\rho_{\infty}=\rho-3\delta\frac{a}{a-1}

and

a=\frac{\rho-\rho_{\infty}}{\rho_{1}-\rho_{\infty}}.

Let us now define

(6.44)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{j}

\displaystyle:={\max{\left\{\frac{\|{{E_{K_{j}}}}\|_{\rho_{j}}}{\gamma^{2}\delta_{j}^{2\tau}},\|{{E_{{W}_{j}}}}\|_{\rho_{j}}\right\}}},

\displaystyle{C_{{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}}

\displaystyle:={\max{\left\{E_{\bar{K}_{j}}a^{2\tau},E_{\bar{W}_{j}}\gamma\delta_{j}\right\}}},

\displaystyle\varkappa_{j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}_{j}},

\displaystyle\kappa_{j}

\displaystyle:=a^{2\tau}{C_{{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}}\varkappa_{j}

and assume (
6.1
) in Lemma
6.1
holds
for the objects up to step
j-1
. Then,

\varkappa_{j}\leq a^{2\tau}{C_{{\mathcal{E}}_{j-1}}}\varkappa_{j-1}^{2}\leq\kappa_{j-1}\varkappa_{j-1}

and

\kappa_{j}\leq a^{2\tau}{C_{{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}}\kappa_{j-1}\varkappa_{j-1}\leq\kappa_{j-1}^{2},

where the last inequality holds as long as
{C_{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}\leq{C_{{\mathcal{E}}_{j-1}}}
.
In order to apply Lemma

6.1
again we need (
6.1
) to hold for
the objects in the step
j
.
Notice that
{C_{\Delta}}
in (
6.2
), as well as the
constants therein, depend on
\delta_{j}
—polynomially by
construction—and on
\hat{\nu}_{j}
and
\nu_{j}
.
See Appendix
A
and definitions
(
6.28
) and (
6.29
). Let us now
assume
\kappa:=\kappa_{0}<1
and
\nu_{0}<1
,
which are included in assumption

3.40
. Recall that
\hat{\nu}\leq\nu
.
Additionally, the assumption
\kappa<1

implies decreasing sequences for

\hat{\nu}_{j},~\nu_{j},~{C_{{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}},~\kappa_{j},~\varkappa_{j},
and
{\mathcal{E}}_{j}
. In particular,

\displaystyle\kappa_{j}\leq\kappa_{j-1}^{2}\leq\kappa^{2^{j}}

(6.45)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{j}\leq\gamma^{2}\delta_{j}^{2\tau}\varkappa_{j}\leq\gamma^{2}\delta_{j}^{2\tau}\kappa_{0}^{2^{j-1}}\varkappa_{0}\leq\left(\frac{\kappa}{a^{2\tau}}\right)^{j}{\mathcal{E}}.

In order to prove convergence, it will be necessary to control the
constants within
{C_{\Delta}}
uniformly with respect to
j
. Hence,
the constants within
{C_{\Delta}}
are taken for the largest values
of
\delta,~\hat{\nu},
and
\nu
, i.e.,
\delta=\delta_{0},~\hat{\nu}=\hat{\nu}_{0}
, and

\nu=\nu_{0}
. Nonetheless, the explicit
\delta
in
{C_{\Delta}}
can
be taken to be the corresponding ones for the step
j
, i.e., they
can be taken to be
\delta=\delta_{j}
.

We will now use estimate (
6.45
) to check condition
(
6.1
) for step
j
.
For the first term in (
6.2
), condition
(
6.1
) reads

{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j}<1

and, using (
6.45
), we have

{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}\left(\frac{\kappa}{a^{2\tau}}\right)^{j}{\mathcal{E}}\leq{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}{\mathcal{E}}<1

where the last inequality holds from the assumption
\nu_{0}<1
.
For the second term in (
6.2
),
let us first compute

(6.46)

\|{K_{j}-K}\|_{\rho_{j}}\leq\sum_{i=0}^{j-1}\|{K_{i+1}-K_{i}}\|_{\rho_{i+1}}\leq C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}{\mathcal{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{j-1}\left(\frac{\kappa}{a^{2\tau}}\right)^{i}\leq\frac{a^{2\tau}}{a^{2\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}{\mathcal{E}}:=\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta K}{\mathcal{E}},

where we used (
6.14
) and (
6.45
). Notice that
the previous bound holds for all
j
—hence, we can obtain
estimate (
3.42
). Similarly, using Lemma

6.1
, we obtain the estimates
(
3.43
)-(
3.45
).
Let us now consider the inequality

\frac{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}_{j}{\mathcal{E}}_{j}}{R-\|{K_{j}-K}\|_{\rho_{j}}}<1

which is satisfied if

\gamma^{2}\delta_{j}^{2\tau}{\mathcal{E}}_{j}+\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta K}{\mathcal{E}}<R

or, alternatively,

\frac{\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}+\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta K}}{R}{\mathcal{E}}<1,

where we used that
\delta_{j}<\delta
and
{\mathcal{E}}_{j}<{\mathcal{E}}
.
It is not hard to show that the previous condition implies
(
6.1
) for the second term in (
6.2
) at
every step; which is included in assumption (
3.40
).
In order to express condition (
6.1
) for the third
term in (
6.2
), we need to control

\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K_{j}}\|_{\rho_{j}}
. Notice that we can recurrently
obtain the bound

\displaystyle\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K_{j}}\|_{\rho_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}+\sum_{i=0}^{j-1}\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta_{i}}{\mathcal{E}}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}+\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\kappa}{a^{2\tau-1}}\right)^{i}{\mathcal{E}}

\displaystyle\leq\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}+\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}a^{2\tau-1}}{\delta(a^{2\tau-1}-\kappa)}{\mathcal{E}}<\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}},

where the last inequality holds if

\frac{dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}}}{\delta(\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}-\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho})}\frac{a^{2\tau-1}}{a^{2\tau-1}-\kappa}{\mathcal{E}}<1,

which is included in assumption (
3.40
). Notice that this
condition guarantees (
6.1
) for the third
term in (
6.2
).
We can proceed similarly and obtain conditions such that
(
6.1
) holds at every step for the remaining
terms in (
6.2
). Such conditions are then included
in (
3.40
) for which we have the explicit constants

(6.47)

\mathfrak{C}:=\max\Bigg\{a^{2\tau}C_{{\mathcal{E}}},\ \mathfrak{C}_{\Delta}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta}:=\max\Bigg\{

\displaystyle{C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$E_{\scalebox{0.65}{\rm symp${P}$}}$}}}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\tilde{\mathcal{U}}}$}}}{R}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}}-\|{{\rm D}_{\theta}K}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}}-\|{{\rm D}_{\varphi}K}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\theta}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}-\|{({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\left({\rm D}_{\varphi}K\right)^{\!\top\!}$}}}-\|{({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}}\gamma\delta^{\tau-1},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\Delta{W}}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}$}}}-\|{{W}}\|_{\rho}},

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{W^{\!\top\!}}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{${W}^{\!\top\!}$}}}-\|{{W}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta{B}}}{{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$B$}}}-\|{{B}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}$}}-\|{\hat{N}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}-\|{\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}}\|_{\rho}},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}-|{{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}}|},

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{C}_{{\Delta\lambda}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda$}}}-|{\lambda}|}\gamma\delta^{\tau},\ \frac{\mathfrak{C}_{{\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}}}{\sigma_{\scalebox{0.65}{{$\lambda^{\scalebox{0.7}{$-1$}}$}}}-|{\lambda^{\!-1}}|}\gamma\delta^{\tau}\Bigg\},

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\tilde{\mathcal{U}}}$}}:=\gamma^{2}\delta^{2\tau}+\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta K},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{\rm D}_{\theta}K$}}:=\frac{a^{2\tau-1}}{a^{2\tau-1}-\kappa}dC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{\rm D}_{\varphi}K$}}:=\frac{a^{2\tau-1}}{a^{2\tau-1}-\kappa}\ell C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}$}}:=\frac{a^{2\tau-1}}{a^{2\tau-1}-\kappa}2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta K}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta({\rm D}_{\varphi}K)^{\!\top\!}$}}:=\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta({\rm D}_{\theta}K)^{\!\top\!}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{${\Delta{W}}$}}:=\frac{a^{\tau}}{a^{\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{W^{\!\top\!}}$}}:=\frac{a^{\tau}}{a^{\tau}-\kappa}2nC_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta{W}}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\Delta{B}}:=\frac{a^{\tau}}{a^{\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{B}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}}:=\frac{a^{\tau}}{a^{\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}}:=\frac{a^{\tau}}{a^{\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta\hat{N}^{\!\top\!}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\scalebox{0.65}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}}:=\frac{a^{\tau}}{a^{\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{$\Delta{\langle{{\mathcal{S}}}\rangle}^{\!-1}$}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{{\Delta\lambda}}:=\frac{a^{2\tau}}{a^{2\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda}$}}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{{\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}}:=\frac{a^{2\tau}}{a^{2\tau}-\kappa}C_{\scalebox{0.75}{${\Delta\lambda^{\!-1}}$}}.

Observe that the first term in (
6.47
) corresponds to
the condition of having a decreasing sequence of errors whereas
the second term corresponds to the conditions necessary for
applying Lemma
6.1
infinitely many times.

7.
